id=\"offerings_update_form_47472_offerings_update_body\"\u003e\n\u003cp\u003eIn January, the producers at the Dr. Phil Primetime show contacted me, seeking a pro-cannabis advocate for their episode entitled, “Is it ‘Just Weed’?” filming the following week in Dallas. \u0026nbsp;I could write a book covering the six days between that call and the moment I walked onto the stage.\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eDr. Phil opens our segment saying, “Our next guest says she uses a marijuana product virtually every day. Jane West is a pro-marijuana advocate and a business owner in the marijuana lifestyle space, now whether it's smoking, a topical or an edible, she uses them all, and she says, it just makes her feel better.”\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eLater I reply, \u0026nbsp;“The vast majority of women in my age group, when they go to the doctor, they are feeling down, they are looking for some solution. \u0026nbsp;Most of them leave with a daily addiction. A bottle of pills they are supposed to take every day - and if it stops working, they are just supposed to take a little bit more. I don’t take prescription medications, I utilize cannabis when I need to, and there are a lot of women that I have found across the country that are finding cannabis is the best solution for them as well.” \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eTo watch the episode, you will need to download the Merit Street media app or tune into Merit+ on Comcast, DirectTV or Dish. \u0026nbsp; I’ll be making the rounds in interviews going into more detail about the experience.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003e\u003cbr\u003e\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eThis appearance is pretty big news for coming weeks. \u0026nbsp;The fact is, legal cannabis businesses in America will continue to produce consumable products with more demand than supply. \u0026nbsp;I am confident in the future of the cannabis industry. \u0026nbsp;As a country, we must to provide opportunity for local businesses more than ever, and our state governments need the tax dollars regulated cannabis sales provide. \u0026nbsp;The cannabis industry in Colorado pumped $302 million tax dollars into Colorado in 2019 alone. \u0026nbsp;Colorado marijuana sales now have hit \u003ca href=\"https://www.denverpost.com/2020/02/18/colorado-marijuana-sales-2019/\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003e$7.79 billion\u003c/a\u003e total since recreational sales began in 2014, generating $1.21 billion in tax revenue. Dispensaries are currently experiencing great demand with people \u003ca href=\"https://www.greenentrepreneur.com/article/347533?_ga=2.3635191.637086247.1584392997-1427567338.1584392997\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003estockpiling cannabis products\u003c/a\u003e. brand names or logos, due to continued federal illegality. \u0026nbsp;Last summer, the USPTO confirmed they would continue to \"refuse registration of marks that show a clear violation of federal law. This includes any products or services related to marijuana, which remains a Schedule I prohibited controlled substance.\" \u0026nbsp;Read more from the experts in this \u003ca href=\"https://www.natlawreview.com/article/uspto-provides-new-guidance-registration-cannabis-and-cannabis-related-marks?utm_source=Shareholders\u0026amp;utm_campaign=165ee1c697-EMAIL_CAMPAIGN_2019_05_03_11_04_COPY_05\u0026amp;utm_medium=email\u0026amp;utm_term=0_8e3c485861-165ee1c697-\u0026amp;mc_cid=165ee1c697\u0026amp;mc_eid=[UNIQID]\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eNational Law Review\u003c/a\u003e article. \u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cp\u003eConsidering the USPTO's stance, our laser-focused licensing model has secured us a strong foundation for a defendable global cannabis brand. \u0026nbsp; Our robust IP portfolio is an advantageous differentiator among the current landscape of cannabis companies. \u0026nbsp;I am proud to inform you that we have won the following registrations we started applications for back in April 2016.\u0026nbsp;\u003c/p\u003e\u003cul\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cp\u003eClass 5: Nutritional supplements in capsule form and liquid form.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cp\u003eClass 34: Smoking pipes; Snuff dispensers; Tobacco water pipes; Electronic cigarette cases; Electronic cigarette holders; Electronic cigarettes; Oral vaporizers for smoking purposes.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cp\u003eClass 35: Advertising services, namely, promoting the brands, goods and services of others; endorsement services, namely, promoting the goods and services of others. On-line retail store services featuring a wide variety of consumer goods of others.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cp\u003eClass 41: Providing information, news and commentary in the field of entertainment, pop culture and recreation and leisure activities; entertainment services, namely, personal appearances by an entrepreneur and business woman.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003cli\u003e\u003cp\u003eClass 44: Providing information in the field of alternative health and healing, wellness and nutrition.\u003c/p\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\u003c/ul\u003e\u003cp\u003eAdditionally, with our US registrations in place, we moved forward last year to apply these registrations to the EU and Canada, and
